# Mana Wash price list — every company and model

Built from the live Mana HQ catalog on 2026-10-18 (`npm run docs:price-list` in apps/api). Prices are
**hub / community**, GST included, at a branch with no price zone. Each model is priced by its **size**: the body type decides the size, and HQ decides
which size each body type is. Luxury brands (BMW, Mercedes-Benz, Audi…) are priced as **Luxury car (XL)**,
35% above a big SUV, for the premium liquids. Two-wheelers have **two prices**: every scooter, moped
and bike is **Regular**; premium bikes (Harley-Davidson, Ducati, Hayabusa, big BMW, Triumph and
Kawasaki) are **Premium**, about double. Scooters are listed apart only because they get no chain care.

**145 companies · 1820 models.** Jump to: [Sizes](#sizes) · [Washes by size](#washes-by-size) ·
[Plans by size](#plans-by-size) · [At home and at communities](#at-home-and-at-communities) ·
[Autos, vans, trucks and machines](#autos-vans-trucks-and-machines) ·
[Cars](#cars-vans-and-commercial-vehicles) · [Two-wheelers](#two-wheelers)

## At home and at communities

Three ways to buy a wash, three ways to price it:

- **Hub** — the customer comes to the shop. The prices above; every other price starts from them.
- **Community** — the team washes many cars at one place on a wash day, so the trip is shared and each car pays less: the community price above.
- **Doorstep** — a wash at the customer’s home: the **hub price + one visit charge per visit**. Two cars at one home pay it once.

### Visit charge

₹49 a visit, then ₹10 for every km (or part of one) after 3 km, measured from the branch, rounded up to end in 9. The team goes up to 15 km.

| Home is | Visit charge | Basic Wash S at home | Basic Wash L at home |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Up to 3 km | ₹49 | ₹499 + ₹49 = **₹548** | ₹799 + ₹49 = **₹848** |
| 5 km | ₹69 | ₹499 + ₹69 = **₹568** | ₹799 + ₹69 = **₹868** |
| 8 km | ₹99 | ₹499 + ₹99 = **₹598** | ₹799 + ₹99 = **₹898** |
| 10 km | ₹119 | ₹499 + ₹119 = **₹618** | ₹799 + ₹119 = **₹918** |
| 12 km | ₹139 | ₹499 + ₹139 = **₹638** | ₹799 + ₹139 = **₹938** |
| 15 km | ₹169 | ₹499 + ₹169 = **₹668** | ₹799 + ₹169 = **₹968** |
| Over 15 km | Not served | — | — |

No busy-time extras yet (HQ › Base prices › Doorstep can add one, like +₹30 on Sunday mornings).

### Bigger communities

No volume discount yet (HQ › Base prices › Community can add steps, like 10+ cars → 10% off).

## Notes

- Community price is for 2 or more cars at the same visit; one car alone pays the hub price.
- A model not in this list (typed by the customer) is priced by the vehicle type they pick.
- “—” means that wash isn’t offered for that vehicle (no Premium Wash for lorries, tractors and JCBs).
- “(off)”: head office has switched that kind of vehicle off (HQ › Vehicles we wash); no branch takes it.
  Its prices are kept for when it’s switched on.
- Plans are for cars and two-wheelers. There are no add-ons on the menu.
- Very muddy vehicles: ₹100–200 extra. Second car on a plan: 10% off the plan.
